<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now read App.tsx lines ~40-240 for modeChip, handleRun, and the state wiring. Let me read that region.

Design (final):
Server:
1. `POST /api/sessions` body gains `mode?: string` and `model?: string`.
   - validate mode ∈ {live, record, replay}; invalid → 400 with clear message.
   - if mode === 'live' or 'record': check live config availability (LLM_BASE_URL/LLM_API_KEY/LLM_MODEL via resolveLiveConfig with model override) → on LlmError 'not-configured' return 400 with the error message.
   - store `llmMode: mode ?? client.mode`, `meta: { source, model: resolvedModel }`.
2. Run handler: build the client for this session from stored row: if `opts.client` injected → use it; else create from session's llmMode + meta.model; and this must be per-run so the app-level `client` is just the default.
   - Need a helper `clientForSession(session)`.
   - `model` passed to runAndPersist must be the session's model (so llm_call records the right model).
   - Also health should expose available modes and default model: add `modes: ['live','record','replay']` and `hasLiveConfig: boolean` (i.e., whether LLM_API_KEY etc. present) so the UI can disable "联网" with an explanation. Let's add `live: { ready: boolean; missing: string[] }`.
3. Add `GET /api/models`? The user asked "能否在页面选择切换模型". A free-text model input is honest and works with any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, but a list would be nicer. We could add an endpoint that lists models from the configured endpoint (GET {baseUrl}/models) — but offline/demo has no base URL → the endpoint returns ready:false and the UI falls back to text input. That's a nice touch: `GET /api/models` returns `{ ready, models: string[], default: string, error? }`, cached briefly? Keep it simple: fetch on demand from the live config; if not configured return ready:false.
   - Live client has `endpointOf` helper. I can implement `listLiveModels()` in live.ts using resolveLiveConfig + fetch GET `${baseUrl}/models`.
   - This is a network call from the server on request — fine, only when user clicks. But careful about timeouts (20s? use 10s).
   - For the UI: a "模型" select populated from /api/models when 联网选中的; plus a free text fallback. To keep it simpler and still useful: dropdown of fetched models + a text input that accepts any id. Hmm, complexity. Maybe: when the user picks 联网真实模型, the UI calls /api/models; if ready, show a `<select>` with those models (plus current default); if not ready, show a hint "没有配置 API Key，先在 .env 里填 LLM_API_KEY（见新手教程）" and keep the field disabled.

That's a good product behavior meeting requirement 5.

Let me scope: implement server (mode/model per session + /api/models + health.live), tests for the new behaviors (invalid mode 400, live without config 400, model stored in meta and used, /api/models when unconfigured). Web: 
- api.ts: CreateSessionInput gains mode/model; health type gains live; add fetchModels.
- App.tsx: state runMode/model; pass to IntakePanel; createSession with them; modeChip reflects chosen mode rather than server default? The chip should reflect the *current selection* (what will run) — good: modeChip(health, selection).
- IntakePanel: new block 「运行模式」 with three chips (离线回放 / 联网真实模型 / 联网并录制卡带?) — user asked live vs replay; record is advanced. Offer 回放 / 联网（真实调用）/ 联网+录制? Keep two + an advanced toggle? I'll offer three chips with hints, defaulting to 回放; for 联网 the model select appears.
- labels: no changes needed for roles.

Also the beginner help (help.ts) may reference modes; maybe add a line about switching in the UI. And screenshots/e2e will need updates (mode chips add a data-shot; e2e scenario 1 may assert header text "离线回放"). Let me check e2e assertions on mode text — e2e scenario list mentions header shows 调用/回放/追问. The mode chip text: for replay "离线回放 · 不联网 · 不需要 API Key". If default selection stays replay, existing assertions hold.

Plan of record: implement item 5 = 页面内切换模式与模型.

Steps:
1. server: llm/live.ts add `listModels(opts)`? Put in index.ts as `listLiveModels`. Timeout 15s. Returns {ready, models, defaultModel, error}.
2. server/http/app.ts: AppOptions gains `clientFactory?: (mode, model) => LlmClient`? Simpler: since createApp already builds default client, add:
   ```ts
   const clientFor = (mode: LlmMode, modelName: string): LlmClient =>
     opts.client ?? createLlmClient({ cassetteDir, mode, model: modelName || undefined, stageModels: reasoning });
   ```
   Wait: options.client injected in tests/demo → always returns injected (tests can't exercise mode switching then; but tests can inject a clientFactory). Add `clientFactory?: (mode: LlmMode, model: string) => LlmClient` option, default as above. Tests inject a factory to assert mode/model plumbing without network.
   - In POST /api/sessions: resolve mode (body.mode) & model (body.model); validate; for live/record call `resolveLiveConfig({model})` to check configuration (catch LlmError → 400).
   - Persist llmMode + meta.model.
   - Run: read session row; use `session.llmMode` and meta.model; build client via factory; pass model to runAndPersist.
   - Session model: `getSession(db, id)` returns row with meta? Need to check repo.getSession return shape — it has meta? SessionSummary includes meta; the DB row likely has meta object. Let me check store/repo.ts getSession.
3. `/api/health` add `live: { ready, missing }` and `modes`.
4. `GET /api/models` → listLiveModels.
5. Tests in packages/server/test/http.test.ts (new describe) + maybe llm test for listLiveModels with fetchImpl stub.
6. web: api.ts + App.tsx + IntakePanel.tsx.
7. e2e: add assertions for mode chips (select 联网 without config → shows the missing-config hint, button disabled? Actually allow creating session? No: server 400s; UI should prevent by hint. Stage 1 — server/src/llm/live.ts: add `listLiveModels`:

```ts
export interface LiveModelList {
  ready: boolean;
  models: string[];
  defaultModel: string;
  error: string | null;
}

/**
 * 从配置好的端点拉一次模型清单，给界面做选择用。
 * 只在用户真的去点「联网」时才调用：默认路径（回放）绝不联网。
 */
export async function listLiveModels(opts: LiveOptions & { fetchImpl?: typeof fetch } = {}): Promise<LiveModelList> {
  let config: LiveConfig;
  try {
    config = resolveLiveConfig(opts);
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    return { ready: false, models: [], defaultModel: '', error: message };
  }
  const doFetch = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
  const url = config.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '') + '/models';
  try {
    const res = await doFetch(url, {
      headers: { authorization: 'Bearer ' + config.apiKey, accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(opts.timeoutMs ?? 15000),
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ready: true, models: [], defaultModel: config.model, error: '模型清单接口返回 ' + String(res.status) };
    const body = (await res.json()) as { data?: unknown };
    const rows = Array.isArray(body?.data) ? body.data : [];
    const models: string[] = [];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const id = recordOf(row)?.['id'];
      if (typeof id === 'string' && id.trim() !== '' && !models.includes(id.trim())) models.push(id.trim());
    }
    return { ready: true, models, defaultModel: config.model, error: null };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    return { ready: false, models: [], defaultModel: config.model, error: '拉取模型清单失败：' + message };
  }
}
```
Note: AbortSignal.timeout in Node 20+. ok.

Hmm careful: `recordOf` is not exported but defined in live.ts — fine, it's in the same file.

Also `ready` semantics: ready = 配置齐全（能联网）。If the models endpoint fails, ready stays true but error set. UI: if !ready → 提示缺配置（禁用选择/给指引）; if ready && models empty → 允许手填。

Now write the listLiveModels function into live.ts after endpointOf (line 72 area). Use edit with exact old_string from lines 67-72. I just read them, so edit is fine.

Insert after endpointOf:

```ts
/** 模型清单：给界面做下拉用。返回 ready=false 表示"没配置好"，此时绝不发起任何网络请求。 */
export interface LiveModelList {
  ready: boolean;
  models: string[];
  defaultModel: string;
  error: string | null;
}

export async function listLiveModels(opts: LiveOptions = {}): Promise<LiveModelList> {
  let config: LiveConfig;
  try {
    config = resolveLiveConfig(opts);
  } catch (err) {
    return { ready: false, models: [], defaultModel: '', error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) };
  }

  const doFetch = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
  const url = config.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '') + '/models';
  try {
    const res = await doFetch(url, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: { authorization: 'Bearer ' + config.apiKey, accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      return { ready: true, models: [], defaultModel: config.model, error: '模型清单接口返回 ' + String(res.status) };
    }
    const body = (await res.json()) as unknown;
    const rows = recordOf(body)?.['data'];
    const models: string[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(rows)) {
      for (const row of rows) {
        const id = recordOf(row)?.['id'];
        if (typeof id === 'string' && id.trim() !== '' && !models.includes(id.trim())) models.push(id.trim());
      }
    }
    return { ready: true, models, defaultModel: config.model, error: null };
  } catch (err) {
    return { ready: true, models: [], defaultModel: config.model, error: '拉取模型清单失败：' + (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)) };
  }
}
```

Careful with String.raw and backticks/dollar-braces: my content has no backticks and no ${. It has `/\/+$/` regex with backslashes — inside String.raw that's fine as long as no backtick. ok.

Also careful: `AbortSignal.timeout` typed OK.

Wait: `opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch` — the type is typeof fetch; fine.

```text
   1 /**
   2  * HTTP 层：一个会话从创建、开跑到读结果的全部接口，追问过程用 SSE 直播。
   3  *
   4  * 三条刻意的取舍：
   5  *   1. 跑沙盘是 POST + SSE 而不是 WebSocket：单向推送用不上双向，
   6  *      而且 SSE 自带 last-event-id 语义，重连按 seq 续传是协议里写好的。
   7  *   2. 同一份提示词与预算在三种模式下完全一致——HTTP 层不参与决策，
   8  *      它只负责把事件搬给前端，把结果交给报告渲染。
   9  *   3. 每个事件都先落库再推送：前端崩了、刷新了，过程也还在，
  10  *      这正是"过程可复现"这个评分维度要的东西。
  11  */
  12 
  13 import { Hono } from 'hono';
  14 import type { Context } from 'hono';
  15 import { streamSSE } from 'hono/streaming';
  16 import { DEFAULT_BUDGET, shortHash } from '@crossexam/shared';
  17 import type { InterviewerRole } from '@crossexam/shared';
  18 import { DEMO_JD, DEMO_RESUME, DEMO_TITLE } from '../demo-content.ts';
  19 import { createLlmClient, defaultCassetteDir, describeClient } from '../llm/index.ts';
  20 import type { LlmClient } from '../llm/types.ts';
  21 import { assembleSession, contractViews, runAndPersist } from '../pipeline.ts';
  22 import type { SessionView } from '../pipeline.ts';
```

```ts
/** 请求里带的运行模式：写错的值明确拒绝，不猜。 */
function modeField(value: unknown): LlmMode | null | undefined {
  const text = textField(value).toLowerCase();
  if (text === '') return undefined;
  return text === 'replay' || text === 'live' || text === 'record' ? (text as LlmMode) : null;
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 联网是否就绪：只查两个真正必需的环境变量。
 * 模型名可以来自界面输入，所以 LLM_MODEL 缺失不算「没配好」——
 * 但会在模型输入框旁提示可以手填。
 */
function liveReadiness(): { ready: boolean; missing: string[] } {
  const missing: string[] = [];
  if ((process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'] ?? '').trim() === '') missing.push('LLM_BASE_URL');
  if ((process.env['LLM_API_KEY'] ?? '').trim() === '') missing.push('LLM_API_KEY');
  return { ready: missing.length === 0, missing };
}
```

```ts
describe('运行模式与模型（页面里逐场选）', () => {
  const ENV_KEYS = ['LLM_BASE_URL', 'LLM_API_KEY', 'LLM_MODEL'] as const;

  function withoutLiveEnv<T>(fn: () => T): T {
    const saved = ENV_KEYS.map((key) => [key, process.env[key]] as const);
    for (const key of ENV_KEYS) delete process.env[key];
    try {
      return fn();
    } finally {
      for (const [key, value] of saved) {
        if (value === undefined) delete process.env[key];
        else process.env[key] = value;
      }
    }
  }
```

```ts
  it('没配好就不发请求：一个网络包都不发，直接说清缺什么', async function () {
    const calls: Call[] = [];
    const listed = await listLiveModels({ fetchImpl: fakeFetch(function () { return jsonResponse({ data: [] }); }, calls) });
```

```ts
describe('运行模式与模型（逐场可选，写进会话行）', () => {
  const ENV_KEYS = ['LLM_BASE_URL', 'LLM_API_KEY', 'LLM_MODEL'];

  async function withoutLiveEnv<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const saved = new Map<string, string | undefined>();
    for (const key of ENV_KEYS) {
      saved.set(key, process.env[key]);
      delete process.env[key];
    }
    try {
      return await fn();
    } finally {
      for (const [key, value] of saved) {
        if (value === undefined) delete process.env[key];
        else process.env[key] = value;
      }
    }
  }

  it('健康检查把三种模式与「联网就绪了吗」一并说清', async () => {
    const t = rig();
    const res = await t.app.request('/api/health');
    const body = (await res.json()) as {
      modes: string[];
      modeLabels: Record<string, string>;
      live: { ready: boolean; missing: string[] };
    };
    expect(body.modes).toEqual(['replay', 'live', 'record']);
    expect(body.modeLabels['replay']).toContain('离线回放');
    expect(Array.isArray(body.live.missing)).toBe(true);
    expect(body.live.ready).toBe(body.live.missing.length === 0);
  });

  it('模式写错就 400：不猜，也不悄悄退回回放', async () => {
    const t = rig();
    const res = await createSession(t, { resumeText: RESUME, mode: 'liv' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(((await res.json()) as { error: string }).error).toContain('运行模式只能是');
  });

  it('没配好联网时当场 400，并点名缺哪个环境变量', async () => {
    await withoutLiveEnv(async () => {
      const t = rig();
      const res = await createSession(t, { resumeText: RESUME, mode: 'live', model: 'deepseek-chat' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
      const error = ((await res.json()) as { error: string }).error;
      expect(error).toContain('LLM_BASE_URL');
      expect(error).toContain('LLM_API_KEY');
    });
  });

  it('回放不需要配置，也不需要模型名——选了回放就把它清掉', async () => {
    await withoutLiveEnv(async () => {
      const t = rig();
      const res = await createSession(t, { resumeText: RESUME, mode: 'replay', model: 'deepseek-chat' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(201);
      const created = (await res.json()) as { id: string; mode: string; model: string };
      expect(created.mode).toBe('replay');
      expect(created.model).toBe('');
      const view = await (await t.app.request('/api/sessions/' + created.id)).json() as { session: { llmMode: string } };
      expect(view.session.llmMode).toBe('replay');
    });
  });

  it('联网那一场会把模式与模型交给客户端工厂：重跑按会话行自己记的来', async () => {
    const saved = { baseUrl: process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'], apiKey: process.env['LLM_API_KEY'] };
    process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'] = 'https://example.invalid/v1';
    process.env['LLM_API_KEY'] = 'test-key';
    try {
      const db = openDatabase({ path: ':memory:' });
      migrate(db);
      opened.push(db);
      const seen: { mode: string; model: string }[] = [];
      const app = createApp({
        db,
        cassetteDir: '.tmp/fixtures',
        clientFactory: (mode, model) => {
          seen.push({ mode, model });
          return scripted();
        },
      });
      const created = await (await app.request('/api/sessions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: JSON_HEADERS,
        body: JSON.stringify({ resumeText: RESUME, jobTitle: '后端工程师', mode: 'live', model: 'deepseek-reasoner' }),
      })).json() as { id: string; mode: string; model: string };
      expect(created.mode).toBe('live');
      expect(created.model).toBe('deepseek-reasoner');

      const run = await app.request('/api/sessions/' + created.id + '/run?stream=0&panel=tech', { method: 'POST' });
      expect(run.status).toBe(200);
      expect(seen).toEqual([{ mode: 'live', model: 'deepseek-reasoner' }]);
    } finally {
      if (saved.baseUrl === undefined) delete process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'];
      else process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'] = saved.baseUrl;
      if (saved.apiKey === undefined) delete process.env['LLM_API_KEY'];
      else process.env['LLM_API_KEY'] = saved.apiKey;
    }
  });

  it('模型清单：没配好时不发请求也要能回答界面', async () => {
    await withoutLiveEnv(async () => {
      const t = rig();
      const res = await t.app.request('/api/models');
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      const body = (await res.json()) as { ready: boolean; models: string[]; missing: string[] };
      expect(body.ready).toBe(false);
      expect(body.models).toEqual([]);
      expect(body.missing).toContain('LLM_API_KEY');
    });
  });
});
```

12 failures in http.test.ts: creating sessions without `mode` now returns 400. Why? Because `modeField(undefined)` → textField → '' → returns undefined; then `const mode = wantedMode ?? client.mode` — scripted client mode is 'live'! The rig injects a scripted client whose mode is 'live', so mode === 'live' → resolveLiveConfig fails → 400.

That's a real design issue: when no mode is given in the request, we must not run the live precheck — we're just inheriting the existing client. The env precheck should only run when the request explicitly asks for live/record.

Fix: only precheck when `wantedMode !== undefined && wantedMode !== 'replay'`. But also: if the app-level client is live (process started with CROSSEXAM_MODE=live) and request omits mode, the config was already validated at startup (bin/serve.ts createLlmClient would throw? Actually createLiveClient doesn't resolve config until a call). Hmm — startup doesn't validate. So a session created with no explicit mode while the process default is live would only fail at run time. Better: precheck when the effective mode !== 'replay' AND the client is being built from env... Simplest correct approach: precheck when effective mode !== 'replay' AND `opts.client === undefined` (i.e., we're not using an injected client). But in the test rig the client IS injected — but a real injected case (demo seed) wouldn't want a 400.

Cleanest semantic: run the precheck when the request explicitly named a non-replay mode. If mode was inherited from the app-level client, config was necessarily present at app construction... no, not true for live.

Alternative cleaner: precheck based on mode !== 'replay' and no injected client override — i.e., `wantedMode !== undefined ? wantedMode !== 'replay' : client.mode !== 'replay' && opts.client === undefined`.

Hmm, that's convoluted. Think about who constructs the app:
- serve.ts: no injected client, no clientFactory → app builds clients via createLlmClient from env; mode comes from CROSSEXAM_MODE.
- tests/demo seed: injected client or factory.

For serve.ts: if env default is live and request omits mode, we want an early 400 with missing names — that's good and matches "once" checked. So condition `mode !== 'replay' && opts.client === undefined` covers both:
- test rig (opts.client injected) → no precheck → 201 ✔
- serve.ts with live default → precheck → 400 naming missing ✔
- request explicitly live with injected client (my new test uses clientFactory, but requires env set → my test sets env) → hmm, with clientFactory and mode 'live' explicitly but env missing: `opts.client === undefined` is true (factory provided, client not) → precheck runs → 400. My test 3 uses rig() (opts.client injected) with explicit live → precheck skipped → 201, breaking my expectation of 400.

Better rule: precheck whenever the *effective* mode is non-replay, EXCEPT when the app was given an explicit client to use... but then rig() with explicit mode 'live' wouldn't 400.

Hmm. What does the user-facing behavior need to be? If someone picks 联网真实模型 in the UI and the server has no key, we must 400 with the missing names — regardless of injected clients (that's a test-only concern).

So the honest fix: rig() should use a client whose mode reflects... The real issue is that the test rig's scripted client claims mode 'live' (it emulates a live client). When the request omits mode, we inherit 'live' and then precheck fails.

So refine: only precheck when the request explicitly asked for a non-replay mode; when inheriting, trust the app's own client (which the app owner configured). What about serve.ts with CROSSEXAM_MODE=live and omitted mode? Then no precheck at session creation; the failure surfaces at run time with the same clear message (stream 'fatal' event) — acceptable, and arguably better: it's the app-owner's配置, not the user's choice. But it'd be nicer to warn at startup instead. Let's add: in serve.ts, if mode !== 'replay', try resolveLiveConfig and print a warning (not fatal) at startup. That covers the operator.

Simplest and most defensible: precheck only when the request explicitly names live/record (user made the choice in the UI). Rule:

```ts
const mode = wantedMode ?? client.mode;
if (wantedMode !== undefined && wantedMode !== 'replay') { precheck }
```

Hmm, to keep prop count sane, group into one prop object: `run: { mode, model, onModeChange, onModelChange, choices, modelOptions, note }`. But existing style is flat props. I'll add flat but concise:

And MODE_CHOICES inside IntakePanel? Better in App since labels come from health.modeLabels. Let me define in IntakePanel:

```ts
  { value: 'live', label: '联网真实模型', hint: '用你自己的模型跑这一场：需要后端配好 LLM_BASE_URL 与 LLM_API_KEY，过程不留卡带' },

```
Wait — labels: health.modeLabels gives server-side Chinese labels. Use those if available, so labels don't drift. I'll use modeLabels from health for the chip labels, with MODE_CHOICES as label fallback. Simplify: MODE_CHOICES define value+hint; label = health.modeLabels?.[value] ?? fallback map. Pass `modeLabels` prop? To keep it simple: pass choices as prop built in App from health (label from health.modeLabels) — then IntakePanel just renders. That keeps single source of truth for labels on the server. But hints live in App then. OK: build the choices array in App (module-level constant with value+hint, labels resolved at render). ### App

State:
```ts

  const [runModel, setRunModel] = useState('');
```

Init: when health loads, set runMode to health.mode? The server default is replay; header chip previously showed health.mode. Now use runMode for the chip. On load, `setRunMode(loadedHealth.mode)` — that mirrors server default. Good.

handler:
```ts
  const handleRunModeChange = useCallback((mode: LlmMode) => {
    setRunMode(mode);
    setModelNote(null);
    setModelOptions([]);
    if (mode === 'replay') return;
    setModelNote('正在向后端要模型清单…');
    void fetchModels()
      .then((list) => {
        setModelOptions(list.models);
        if (runModelRef.current === '' && list.defaultModel !== '') setRunModel(list.defaultModel);
        if (!list.ready) {
          setModelNote('后端还没配好联网：缺 ' + list.missing.join('、') + '。补上 .env 里的这两个变量再刷新页面。');
        } else if (list.error !== null) {
          setModelNote(list.error);
        } else {
          setModelNote(list.models.length === 0 ? '后端没报错，但清单是空的：直接手填模型名。' : null);
        }
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => setModelNote(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '模型清单拉取失败'));
  }, []);
```

```ts
function modeChip(mode: LlmMode | null, health: HealthInfo | null): { text: string; className: string } {
  if (health === null || mode === null) return { text: '连接本地服务中…', className: 'opacity-70' };
  if (mode === 'replay') return { text: '离线回放 · 不联网、不需要 API Key', className: 'border-mint-500/40 text-mint-400' };
  if (mode === 'record') return { text: '联网录制中 · 会把这一场存成卡带', className: 'border-amber-ink/40 text-amber-ink' };
  return { text: '联网直连 · 用你自己的模型跑', className: 'border-ember-500/40 text-ember-300' };
}
```

```
// 运行模式与模型：默认离线回放（不联网），切到联网才出现模型输入框并向后端要清单。
const modeChips = await evalJs('(() => { const pick = (n) => document.querySelector("[data-shot=" + n + "]") !== null; return [pick("mode-replay"), pick("mode-live"), pick("mode-record")].join(","); })()');
check('投料台给出三种运行模式', modeChips === 'true,true,true', modeChips);
const replayNote = await evalJs('(document.querySelector("[data-shot=mode-note]") || { textContent: "" }).textContent');
const modelHidden = await evalJs('document.querySelector("[data-shot=model-input]") === null');
check('默认离线回放：写明不联网，且不显示模型输入框', replayNote.indexOf('不联网') >= 0 && modelHidden === true, replayNote.slice(0, 40) + ' / ' + String(modelHidden));
await evalJs("document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"mode-live\"]').click()");
let liveNote = '';
for (let i = 0; i < 30; i += 1) {
  liveNote = await evalJs('(document.querySelector("[data-shot=mode-note]") || { textContent: "" }).textContent');
  if ((await evalJs('document.querySelector("[data-shot=model-input]") !== null')) === true && liveNote.length > 0) break;
  await sleep(200);
}
check('切到联网后出现模型输入框并选好模型', liveNote.length > 0, liveNote.slice(0, 50));
check('没配置联网时明说缺哪个环境变量，而不是静默失败', liveNote.indexOf('LLM_') >= 0, liveNote.slice(0, 60));
await evalJs("document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"mode-replay\"]').click()");
await sleep(300);
const backNote = await evalJs('(document.querySelector("[data-shot=mode-note]") || { textContent: "" }).textContent');
const modelHiddenAgain = await evalJs('document.querySelector("[data-shot=model-input]") === null');
check('切回离线回放后模型输入框收起', backNote.indexOf('不联网') >= 0 && modelHiddenAgain === true, backNote.slice(0, 30));
```

```text
 587 ## F40. proc.kill() 杀不掉 Edge，残留浏览器会让自动化脚本静默连错人（2026-10-26）
 588 
 589 给界面点测加导入场景之后，`npm run e2e:ui` 从第 6 项开始连续 FAIL：「第一次打开自动弹出新手教程」失败，
 590 随后在 e2e.mjs:199 抛 `TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'click')`——遮罩按钮不存在。
 591 产品与构建都没问题（干净 profile 下实测教程确实会弹）：**脚本连上的是上一轮遗留的浏览器。**
 592 
 593 根因有两层：
 594 
 595 1. 两个浏览器工具都用 `proc.kill()` 收尾。Windows 上这只结束启动器进程，Edge 的进程树还活着，
 596    并继续占着 `--remote-debugging-port`。实测 9344 端口上**同时有两个监听者**——用 pwsh 按
 597    `CommandLine -match 'remote-debugging-port'` 一次清出 57 个孤儿 Edge 进程。
 598 2. 脚本连 CDP 时只找「第一个 type=page 的 target」，不检查它是谁。旧浏览器的 profile 里
 599    `crossexam.seen-help=1` 早就写过了，于是「首开自动弹教程」必然失败——这不是 flaky，是必然。
 600 
 601 处置（三件事一起做，缺一条都会复发）：
 602 
 603 - **收尾用 CDP 的 Browser.close**：拿 /json/version 的 webSocketDebuggerUrl 建一条浏览器级连接发 Browser.close，
 604   这才是「关掉这个浏览器」的正解；`taskkill /pid <pid> /T /F` 只当兜底（killTree）。
 605 - **起浏览器前预检调试端口**：端口被占用就响亮失败并打印占用者，绝不静默连上去。
 606 - **只认刚启动的空窗口**：/json/list 里挑 `type === 'page' && url === 'about:blank'` 的 target；
控制台/日志问题：无
```

```
async function lines(file){ const r = await tools.read({file_path:file, offset:1, limit:5000}); return r.lines.map(l=>l.text); }
async function replaceLine(file, needle, build){ const ls = await lines(file); const hit = ls.filter(t=>t.includes(needle)); if(hit.length!==1) throw new Error('anchor not unique: '+needle); await tools.edit({file_path:file, old_string:hit[0], new_string:build(hit[0])}); }
async function appendTail(file, text, tailLines=3){ const ls=await lines(file); const tail=ls.slice(-tailLines).join('\n'); ... }
```

```
## Session 20 · 2026-10-26 · 第 5 项：页面里自己选离线回放还是联网模型

**目标**：用户问「live 模型与离线回放的区别是什么，能不能在页面里选择切换模型」。这不只是加个开关——
它要求「这一场用什么跑」变成可留档、可解释、可复现的一件事。

- 后端：`LiveOptions` 上加 `listLiveModels()`（先 resolveLiveConfig，配不全就**一个网络包都不发**直接返回原因）；
  `AppOptions` 加 `clientFactory`；`POST /api/sessions` 接受 `mode` 与 `model`，落进会话行的 `llmMode` 与 `meta.model`；
  跑的时候按**会话行**建客户端（单一事实来源在库里，不用内存 Map）；新增 `GET /api/models`。
- 前端：投料台加「运行模式」三选一（离线回放 / 联网直连 / 联网录制）+ 模型 `input + datalist`（可下拉可手填）；
  只切到联网才去拉清单，拉不到也给出下一步文案（缺哪个环境变量 / 接口返回什么），不静默失败。
- 门禁：`npm run verify` 全绿（36 个测试文件 / 369 条断言 + 冒烟 9 项 + build）；`npm run e2e:ui` 47/47；
  `npm run shots` 15 张（新增 15-modes.png），控制台 0 报错，跑完孤儿 Edge 0 个。

### 一次真缺陷（在点测里抓到的）

e2e 里「没配置联网就明说缺哪个环境变量」先失败后成功：等待条件写的是「模型输入框出现且提示非空」，
而那一刻提示还是 `正在向后端要模型清单…`——**元素在点击瞬间就出现了，结论是几百毫秒后异步回来的**。
改成「提示里不再有那句过程文案」再断言。见 findings F41。顺带把两个浏览器脚本继承来的 `LLM_*` 显式抹掉，
让「没配好联网」这条分支不随本机环境变化。

### 下一步

12 项里的其余项：自定义面试官 + 用 API 优化自己的契约（含非计算机专业预设）→ 确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC →
零门槛安装（含桌面快捷方式，需用户同意）→ 可增加功能清单 → 全部完成后再次全功能测试。录像仍留到最后。
```

```
| D33 | 运行模式逐场选，模式与模型落在**会话行**上，而不是进程启动参数 | 「这一场用什么跑」是实验条件，必须与结果一起留档，否则翻回历史会话时无法解释它当时是怎么跑出来的；因此 `POST /api/sessions` 收 mode/model 并落 `llmMode` + `meta.model`，跑的时候按会话行建客户端（单一事实来源在库里，不用内存 Map）。只有请求**显式**写了非 replay 模式才做联网预检——第一版按「生效模式」判断，把测试里注入的脚本化客户端（mode=live）也拦下来了，12 条既有测试全红 |
| D34 | 模型清单拉不到就让用户手填，且离线路径一个网络包都不发 | `/models` 不是所有自建网关都实现，而且它**不是可用性证据**（本机 GET /v1/models 只列两款模型，实际能调通四款）；所以前端用 `input + datalist`，清单为空 / 接口非 2xx / 没配环境变量三种情况都给下一步文案。离线回放默认不拉清单，保证评委那条路径零网络依赖 |
```

```
### 3.6.5 运行模式与模型：逐场选，且随会话留档

三种模式（离线回放 / 联网直连 / 联网录制）由**页面逐场选择**，选择结果写进会话行：`llmMode` 落模式，
`meta.model` 落模型名。跑的时候按会话行建客户端——单一事实来源在库里，不需要内存里的映射表，
翻回一周前的会话也能说清它当时是怎么跑出来的。

`GET /api/models` 只在显式选择联网后才被前端调用：先 `resolveLiveConfig`，缺 `LLM_BASE_URL` / `LLM_API_KEY`
就**一个网络包都不发**，直接把缺哪个变量告诉界面。拉不到清单不等于不能用——界面是 `input + datalist`，
可以手填模型名；`/models` 返回什么也**不是可用性证据**（本机实测它只列出两款，而实际能调通四款）。
见 D33、D34、F41。
```

Requirements (m04531 #4): "不能只有技术面试官，要能在页面里自己设置面试官，并通过 API 对自己设置的面试官契约进行优化，要求所有专业的人都能用（不只计算机）"

So: 
(a) 页面内可自定义面试官阵容：角色名、职责/关注点、语气、资深度等，并生成对应意图契约（结构化 JSON），可保存/复用。
(b) 通过 API 对自己设置的面试官契约进行优化：即用户写一段自然语言描述 → 调 LLM（联网模式）把契约优化成结构化 contract（goal/tactics/persona）；离线模式要有确定性回退（否则评委没 Key 也能看到？）。要求可解释、可版本化、可 diff、可与运行时对账（创新点 3）。
(c) 不限于计算机专业：预设里要有非技术岗位（如市场/财务/教师/医护/公务员/设计等），并且提示词不能默认编程。

- shared: 新增 `contract-draft.ts`（或扩展 contract.ts）:
  - `ContractDraft` zod schema（模型输出）：`{ persona: {name, role, seniority, tone, blurb}, goal, tactics: string[], focus?: string[], domain?: string }`
  - `buildContractFromDraft(draft, version)` → `AgentContract`（补齐 id/digest）
  - 校验：tactics 数量 2-5，每条长度限制，禁止空；persona.role 等字段长度上限。
- server:
  - `orchestrator/prompts.ts`: `CONTRACT_OPTIMIZER_SYSTEM` + `contractOptimizeRequest(...)`（purpose 'contract'，不进入卡带必录？要走 LLM 客户端以便 record/replay）。
  - `orchestrator/optimize-contract.ts`: `optimizeContract(client, input, opts)` → 调 LLM → zod 校验 → 返回 draft + 说明 diff（相对输入改了什么的自然语言说明？）
  - 新增 purpose 'contract'，stageRequest 处理；stage settings。
  - HTTP: `POST /api/contracts/draft` body { description, role?, seniority?, tone?, domain?, panel? } → 返回优化后的契约 + 原始 draft + 校验通过的字段；离线模式（replay）没有卡带 → 会 cassette-missing；所以需要离线确定性回退：`localDraft(description)` 生成契约（不联网），并标注 `source: 'local' | 'llm'`。这样无 Key 也能用（重要：评委零门槛 + 用户要求 7）。

  - `POST /api/contracts/optimize` 也许叫 optimize。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Good. Now write shared/src/interviewer.ts. Also widen Persona.role to `InterviewerRole | 'custom'`.

Design of interviewer.ts:

```ts
/**
 * 自定义面试官（P7 第 4 项）：让用户自己定义一位面试官，并用 API 把这段描述优化成结构化意图契约。
 * 为什么不是「让用户直接写 system prompt」：写 prompt 的人多半写不出一份可评审的 prompt……
 * 领域无关：提示词与本地兜底都不假设候选人是程序员。
 */
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Persona, Seniority, Tone } from './domain.ts';
import { COMMON_CONSTRAINTS, COMMON_FORBIDDEN, type ContractBudgetView, type IntentContract } from './contract.ts';
import { shortHash } from './util.ts';

export const SENIORITY_VALUES = ['junior','mid','senior','principal'] as const;
export const TONE_VALUES = ['neutral','skeptical','warm','blunt'] as const;

export const interviewerDraftSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1).max(24),
  domain: z.string().max(40),
  seniority: z.enum(SENIORITY_VALUES),
  tone: z.enum(TONE_VALUES),
  blurb: z.string().min(4).max(60),
  goal: z.string().min(8).max(200),
  tactics: z.array(z.string().min(4).max(120)).min(2).max(5),
});

export type InterviewerDraft = z.infer<typeof interviewerDraftSchema>;

export const CUSTOM_ID_PREFIX = 'custom-';
export function customInterviewerId(name: string, salt = ''): string  // slug: 非字母数字 → '-', 小写; 空则 'custom'; 加 shortHash(name+salt,4) 保唯一
export function buildCustomContract(input: { id: string; version?: number; draft: InterviewerDraft; budget: ContractBudgetView }): IntentContract
export function draftDigest(draft) → shortHash(stableStringify(draft), 16)
export interface DraftDiff { field: string; before: string; after: string }
export function diffDrafts(before: InterviewerDraft, after: InterviewerDraft): DraftDiff[]
export const DOMAIN_HINTS: readonly string[]  // 用于页面提示：列举一堆专业
export function localDraftFromText(text: string, opts?: Partial<InterviewerDraft>): InterviewerDraft
export function localDraftNotes(text, draft): string[]  // 说明离线生成用了哪些规则
```

localDraftFromText implementation details (deterministic, no time):
- name: opts.name ?? 从文本里第一条匹配的行业词 + '面试官'（如「市场营销面试官」），否则 '自定义面试官'
- domain: opts.domain ?? 匹配到的行业词 ?? ''
- seniority: opts.seniority ?? 'senior'; tone: opts.tone ?? 'neutral'
- goal: 从领域生成：「在<domain>方向的招聘语境里，验证简历里与<domain>相关的表述是否真的站得住。」; 若文本里有句子（长度>=8 的第一句）则用之作为 goal（截断 120）
- blurb: '<domain>方向的面试官，按这一行的实际标准看简历' 或默认
- tactics: 由规则命中拼，去重，至少 3（≤5）：
  - 数字/百分比/倍 → '把简历里的数字拆开问：怎么测出来的、口径是什么、对比基准是什么。'
  - 负责/主导/带领/牵头 → '追问「你负责」与「你参与」的边界：这件事里哪一部分是你独立完成的。'
  - 团队/协作/配合/跨部门 → '追问协作细节：对方是谁、你提了什么、最后按谁的意见改的。'
  - 学习/熟悉/了解/掌握 → '对「熟悉/了解」这类词追一层：最近一次用它解决具体问题是什么时候。'
  - 时间/年月/至今 → '把两段经历的时间线摆在一起，问衔接处的空档与变化原因。'
  - 获奖/优秀/第一 → '追问成果的评选范围与你的实际贡献占比。'
  - 兜底三条通用（行业无关）：
    - '要求为简历里最笼统的那句话举一个具体例子：时间、对象、你做了什么。'
    - '追问这件事如果重做一次会改什么，用来分辨真实经历与照抄描述。'
    - '追问这件事与目标岗位的相关性：为什么这件事能说明你适合。'
- If text mentions a question style e.g. "压力" → tone 'blunt'。 "温和/友好" → warm。 "细节/技术/深挖" → skeptical.

Keep it reasonable: implementation ~120 lines.

```text
  17     counters.set(prefix, n);
```

CONTRACT_OPTIMIZER_SYSTEM:
```
你是「面试官设计师」。用户会用一句人话描述他想要什么样的面试官，你要把它变成一份结构化的面试官契约草稿。

## 你要产出什么
- name：这位面试官的称呼（2-12 字，例如「市场总监」「三甲医院护理部主管」）
- domain：面试官所在专业领域（不超过 20 字；用户没提就按目标岗位推断）
- seniority：junior / mid / senior / principal
- tone：neutral / skeptical / warm / blunt
- blurb：一句话自我介绍（4-60 字）
- goal：这一场你想问出什么（8-200 字，要具体到「判断候选人哪一项能力」）
- tactics：2-5 条追问手法，每条 4-120 字，必须是可执行的动作（例如「听到百分比就追问分母是多少」）

## 纪律
1. 只输出 JSON，不要解释。
2. **不要假设用户是程序员**：用户可能来自医护、财会、教师、公考、外贸等行业，tactics 要用该行业的行话。
3. tactics 必须是「怎么问」，不是「问什么」。不要把某一条简历主张写死进去——这位面试官要能被用在任何一份简历上。
4. 不要编造用户没提供的信息；拿不准就按目标岗位推断并保持宽泛。
```

```text
   4  * 这里是"多 Agent 对抗"真正的样子：抽取 → N 轮并发追问 → 作答 → 判定 → 反推建议。

   7  * 走的是**同一条代码路径**，差别只在客户端从哪儿拿文本。
   8  * 数据库与 SSE 由调用方接在 onEvent 上——回放时事件序列逐字节可比。

  11  *   1. round 上限（编排层的保险丝）
  12  *   2. 预算账本（每主张轮数、每面试官额度、全场总次数；这是真正的闸门）
  13  *   3. 一轮下来没有任何新追问就停（模型无话可说时不必空转）
  14  * 因此"死循环"不是被提示词求着别发生，而是在设计上不存在。
```

Panel module design (server):

```ts
/**
 * 阵容解析：把「页面上选的面试官 id 列表」变成一份可以开跑的契约列表。
 * ...
 */
import { buildContracts } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { BudgetConfig, ContractBudgetView, IntentContract, InterviewerRole } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { getInterviewer } from '../store/index.ts';
import type { Db } from '../store/index.ts';
import { DEFAULT_PANEL } from './run.ts';

export const PRESET_ROLES: readonly InterviewerRole[] = ['tech','hr','hiring_manager','stress','domain_expert'];

export function isPresetRole(id: string): id is InterviewerRole {
  return (PRESET_ROLES as readonly string[]).indexOf(id) >= 0;
}

export function budgetView(config: BudgetConfig): ContractBudgetView {
  return {
    maxRoundsPerClaim: config.maxRoundsPerClaim,
    maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim: config.maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim,
    maxTotalProbes: config.maxTotalProbes,
  };
}

export interface ResolvedPanel {
  /** 上场的面试官 id，顺序就是页面上的顺序 */
  ids: string[];
  contracts: IntentContract[];
  /** 找得到的自定义面试官 id —— 用于告诉用户「这场里有几位是你自己配的」 */
  customIds: string[];
  /** 库里已经不存在的 id：调用方必须拒绝开跑，而不是悄悄少一位 */
  missing: string[];
}

export function resolvePanel(db: Db, ids: readonly string[], budget: BudgetConfig): ResolvedPanel {
  const preset = buildContracts(ids.filter(isPresetRole), budget, 1);
  const byId = new Map<string, IntentContract>();
  for (const c of preset) byId.set(c.id, c);
  const missing: string[] = [];
  const customIds: string[] = [];
  for (const id of ids) {
    if (isPresetRole(id) || byId.has(id)) continue;
    const row = getInterviewer(db, id);
    if (row === null) { missing.push(id); continue; }
    // 预算以**这一场**的配置为准：库里的契约留着当初保存时的预算，那是历史，不是运行时。
    byId.set(id, { ...row.contract, budget: budgetView(budget) });
    customIds.push(id);
  }
  const contracts: IntentContract[] = [];
  for (const id of ids) { const c = byId.get(id); if (c !== undefined) contracts.push(c); }
  return { ids: contracts.map((c) => c.id), contracts, customIds, missing };
}

/** 预设阵容的契约（不需要数据库）。页面在开跑之前预览契约用这个。 */
export function presetPanel(ids, budget): IntentContract[]
export function panelIdsOf(panel: readonly string[] | undefined): string[]  // dedupe + fallback DEFAULT_PANEL
export function viewOf(contract): ContractView-like?  -- maybe in pipeline.
```

```ts
}
```
